     1	/**
     2	 * contracts/r3.0e/experiment-contract.js — R3.0E E1 · Experiment shape contract (NON-PRODUCTION).
     3	 *
     4	 * Defines the structural shape of an Experiment object. Mirrors the D1 pattern: closed key
     5	 * set + closed-enum status + mandatory field presence + bounded strings/arrays + no causal
     6	 * overclaim at the structural layer (E3 outcome classifier enforces semantic invariants).
     7	 *
     8	 * Experiment is a R3.0E-owned object stored in a SEPARATE versioned IndexedDB namespace
     9	 * at E2_EXPERIMENT_STORE. It is NEVER an extension of the frozen R3.0B case-record schema
    10	 * (per SKYLINE D12/E1 ruling). The follow-up linkage between Experiment and Case is
    11	 * BIDIRECTIONAL but lives in a separate linkage object.

    25	    'schemaVersion',
    26	    'experimentId',                  // 'exp_<16hex>'
    27	    'sourceCaseId',                  // R3.0B case-record caseId (string ref only — no schema extension)
    28	    'sourceHypothesisId',            // R3.0D hypothesisId
    29	    'sourceRecommendationId',        // R3.0D priorityId or recommendation reference
    30	    'targetMetric',                  // canonical channel id (e.g., 'roll_gradient_deg_per_g')

    32	    'expectedDirection',             // 'increase' | 'decrease' | 'no_change'

    69	  function _utf8Bytes(s) { try { return (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8'); } catch (e) { return (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length * 4 : 0; } }

   133	          if (!scKeyOk) { reasons.push(CODES.EXPERIMENT_STOP_CONDITIONS_MISSING); break; }
   134	          if (!_nonEmptyStr(sc.i18nKey)) { reasons.push(CODES.EXPERIMENT_STOP_CONDITIONS_MISSING); break; }

   142

   146	      // post-applied stage. We allow non-null outcome only for completed/abandoned/invalid

   162	      } catch (eAny) { reasons.push(CODES.EXPERIMENT_INVALID); }

    72	    var seen = Object.create(null);
    73	    if (Array.isArray(reasonCodes)) {
    74	      for (var i = 0; i < reasonCodes.length; i++) {
    75	        var rc = reasonCodes[i];
    76	        if (typeof rc === 'string' && !seen[rc]) { seen[rc] = true; codes.push(rc); }
    77	      }
    78	    }
    79	    if (codes.length === 0) codes.push(REASON_CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION);
    80	    return Object.freeze({
    81	      eligible: false,
    82	      status: 'blocked',
    83	      reasonCodes: Object.freeze(codes),
    84	      explanationKeys: Object.freeze(codes.map(function (c) { return 'r3_0e.reason.' + c.toLowerCase(); })),
    85	      detail: typeof opts.detail === 'string' ? opts.detail : '',
    86	      result: null,
    87	    });
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  // ---- Structural helpers shared by every R3.0E contract module ----
    91	  function isOriginalPlainObject(v) {
    92	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return false;
    93	    try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); return p === Object.prototype || p === null; }
    94	    catch (e) { return false; }
    95	  }
    96	  function hasHiddenOwnKey(o) {
    97	    try {
    98	      var syms = Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(o);
    99	      if (syms && syms.length > 0) return true;
   100	      var names = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(o);
   101	      for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
   102	        var d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(o, names[i]);
   103	        if (!d || d.enumerable !== true) return true;
   104	        if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') return true;
   105	      }
   106	      return false;
   107	    } catch (e) { return true; }
   108	  }
   109	  function hasNonPlainNestedObject(o) {
   110	    // Codex E1-R1-01 closure: recursive descriptor audit. Walks every nested object/array
   111	    // descriptor, rejecting Symbol-keyed own keys, non-enumerable own keys, accessor
   112	    // (get/set) descriptors, non-plain prototypes (class instances / Proxy targets /
   113	    // arbitrary class), and sparse-array hole patterns.
   114	    //
   115	    // Codex E1-R2-01 closure: traversal overflow MUST fail closed (return true). Previous
   116	    // code returned false (clean) when the visit cap was hit, which let a hostile attacker
   117	    // hide a Symbol-keyed / accessor / non-plain object beyond node 4096. The walk now
   118	    // tracks both depth and node count; overflow on either dimension → reject. Depth is
   119	    // tracked per-node via parallel stack so depth>32 also triggers reject.
   120	    try {
   121	      var MAX_DEPTH = 32;
   122	      var MAX_NODES = 4096;
   123	      var stack = [{ v: o, depth: 0 }];
   124	      var visited = 0;
   125	      while (stack.length > 0) {
   126	        if (visited >= MAX_NODES) return true; // fail-closed on node overflow
   127	        var frame = stack.shift(); visited++;
   128	        if (frame.depth > MAX_DEPTH) return true; // fail-closed on depth overflow
   129	        var v = frame.v;
   130	        if (v === null) continue;
   131	        var t = typeof v;
   132	        if (t === 'function' || t === 'symbol' || t === 'bigint') return true;
   133	        if (t !== 'object') continue;
   134	        var isArr = Array.isArray(v);
   135	        if (!isArr) {
   136	          var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
   137	          if (!(p === Object.prototype || p === null)) return true;
   138	        } else {
   139	          var p2 = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);
   140	          if (p2 !== Array.prototype) return true;
   141	        }
   142	        // Symbol-keyed own keys → reject at any nesting depth.
   143	        var syms;
   144	        try { syms = Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(v); } catch (e) { return true; }
   145	        if (syms && syms.length > 0) return true;
   146	        var names = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(v);
   147	        for (var j = 0; j < names.length; j++) {
   148	          var name = names[j];
   149	          // Array.length is non-enumerable own — skip it from the audit while still
   150	          // walking indexed entries below.
   151	          if (isArr && name === 'length') continue;
   152	          var d;
   153	          try { d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v, name); } catch (eDesc) { return true; }
   154	          if (!d) return true;
   155	          if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') return true;
   156	          if (d.enumerable !== true) return true;
   157	          stack.push({ v: d.value, depth: frame.depth + 1 });
   158	        }
   159	      }
   160	      return false;
   161	    } catch (e) { return true; }
   162	  }
   163	  function toCleanCopy(o) {
   164	    try {
   165	      if (typeof structuredClone === 'function') return structuredClone(o);
   166	      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
   167	    } catch (e) { return null; }
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  var api = {
   171	    REASON_CODES: REASON_CODES,
   172	    ALL_REASON_CODES: ALL_REASON_CODES,
   173	    isReasonCode: isReasonCode,
   174	    buildBlockedResult: buildBlockedResult,
   175	    isOriginalPlainObject: isOriginalPlainObject,
   176	    hasHiddenOwnKey: hasHiddenOwnKey,
   177	    hasNonPlainNestedObject: hasNonPlainNestedObject,
   178	    toCleanCopy: toCleanCopy,
   179	  };
   180	  Object.freeze(api);
   181	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
   182	  if (root) {
   183	    try { Object.defineProperty(root, 'R3_0E_ReasonCodes', { value: api, writable: false, enumerable: false, configurable: false }); }
   184	    catch (e) { root.R3_0E_ReasonCodes = api; }
   185	  }
   186	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

     5	 * Uses the R3.0B storage-backend.js contract for atomic transactions:
     6	 *   backend.transact({ stores:[ns...], reads:[{store,key}...], compute:(readValues)=>({writes, result}) })

     8	 * Per SKYLINE D12/E1 ruling: R3.0E persistence lives in SEPARATE versioned stores and MUST
     9	 * NOT extend the frozen R3.0B portable case-record schema body.
    10	 *
    11	 * Stores:
    12	 *   - r3_0e_experiments       payload by experimentId
    13	 *   - r3_0e_experimentsIndex  summary index by experimentId
    14	 *   - r3_0e_outcomes          payload by outcomeId
    15	 *   - r3_0e_outcomesIndex     summary index by outcomeId
    16	 *   - r3_0e_timelines         payload by caseId (timeline-per-case append-only doc)
    17	 *   - r3_0e_followupLinks     payload by linkId

    19	 *   - r3_0e_storeMetadata     migration / schema-version marker
    20	 *
    21	 * Persistence semantics:
    22	 *   - Every payload validated by its E1 contract BEFORE write (in compute()).
    23	 *   - Every payload re-validated on read; future-schemaVersion → fail-closed reject.

   240	            return { writes: [], result: _deepFreeze(rec) };
   241	          },
   242	        });
   243	      },
   244	      appendEvent: function (caseId, event) {
   245	        return backend.transact({
   246	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES],
   247	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES, key: caseId }],
   248	          compute: function (reads) {
   249	            var existing = reads[0] || { schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline, caseId: caseId, events: [] };
   250	            if (existing.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   251	            for (var i = 0; i < existing.events.length; i++) {
   252	              if (existing.events[i].eventId === event.eventId) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_DUPLICATE_EVENT');
   253	            }
   254	            if (existing.events.length > 0) {
   255	              var prevMs = Date.parse(existing.events[existing.events.length - 1].createdAt);
   256	              var newMs = Date.parse(event.createdAt);
   257	              if (isNaN(newMs) || newMs < prevMs) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_OUT_OF_ORDER');
   258	            }
   259	            var nextEvents = existing.events.concat([event]);
   260	            var next = { schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline, caseId: caseId, events: nextEvents };
   261	            var v = TL.validateCaseTimelineShape(next);
   262	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   263	            return { writes: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES, key: caseId, value: next }], result: event.eventId };
   264	          },
   265	        });

   311	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
   312	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: linkId }],
   313	          compute: function (reads) {
   314	            return { writes: [], result: _validateOne(reads[0]) };
   315	          },
   316	        });
   317	      },
   318	      listForParent: function (parentCaseId) {
   319	        // 1. Read the reverse index.
   320	        return backend.transact({
   321	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE],
   322	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: parentCaseId }],
   323	          compute: function (reads) {
   324	            var idx = reads[0];
   325	            // Codex E2-R2-01 closure: validate the reverse-index row itself.
   326	            if (idx === undefined || idx === null) return { writes: [], result: [] };
   327	            if (idx.parentCaseId !== parentCaseId) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { detail: 'reverse-index parentCaseId mismatch' });
   328	            if (!Array.isArray(idx.linkIds)) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { detail: 'reverse-index linkIds not array' });
   329	            return { writes: [], result: idx.linkIds.slice() };
   330	          },
   331	        }).then(function (linkIds) {
   332	          if (!linkIds.length) return [];
   333	          // 2. Read every link in one transact (atomic snapshot).
   334	          var readSpec = linkIds.map(function (id) { return { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: id }; });
   335	          return backend.transact({
   336	            stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
   337	            reads: readSpec,
   338	            compute: function (reads) {
   339	              // Codex E2-R1-02 closure: validate every fetched link before returning.
   340	              // Codex E2-R2-01 closure: ALSO verify each validated link's parentCaseId
   341	              // matches the caller-supplied parentCaseId. A poisoned reverse index that
   342	              // points at another parent's valid link is rejected as corruption rather
   343	              // than silently leaked across parent boundaries.
   344	              var out = [];
   345	              for (var i = 0; i < reads.length; i++) {
   346	                var validated = _validateOne(reads[i]);
   347	                if (validated === null) continue;
   348	                if (validated.parentCaseId !== parentCaseId) {
   349	                  throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { detail: 'reverse-index points at link with mismatched parentCaseId' });
   350	                }
   351	                out.push(validated);
   352	              }
   353	              return { writes: [], result: out };
   354	            },
   355	          });
   356	        });
   357	      },
   358	      markParentStatus: function (linkId, newStatus) {
   359	        if (['present', 'archived', 'deleted'].indexOf(newStatus) === -1) {
   360	          return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_LINK_PARENT_STATUS_INVALID'));
   361	        }
   362	        return backend.transact({
   363	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
   364	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: linkId }],
   365	          compute: function (reads) {
   366	            var cur = reads[0];
   367	            if (cur === undefined || cur === null) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_MISSING');
   368	            var next = Object.assign({}, cur, { parentStatus: newStatus });
   369	            var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(next);
   370	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   371	            return { writes: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: linkId, value: next }], result: linkId };
   372	          },
   373	        });
   374	      },
   375	    };
   376	  }
   377	
   378	  // ========================================================================================
   379	  // Store metadata
   380	  // ========================================================================================
   381	  function createStoreMetadata(backend) {
   382	    if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
   383	    return {
   384	      readVersion: function () {
   385	        return backend.transact({
   386	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.STORE_METADATA],
   387	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.STORE_METADATA, key: '__r3_0e_version' }],
   388	          compute: function (reads) {
   389	            return { writes: [], result: reads[0] || null };
   390	          },
   391	        });
   392	      },
   393	      writeVersion: function (versionMap) {
   394	        if (!versionMap || typeof versionMap !== 'object') return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_VERSION_INVALID'));
   395	        return backend.transact({
   396	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.STORE_METADATA],
   397	          reads: [],
   398	          compute: function () {
   399	            return {
   400	              writes: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.STORE_METADATA, key: '__r3_0e_version', value: { versions: versionMap, updatedAt: _now() } }],
   401	              result: true,
   402	            };
   403	          },
   404	        });
   405	      },
   406	    };
   407	  }
   408	
   409	  var api = {
   410	    STORE_NAMES: STORE_NAMES,
   411	    SCHEMA_VERSIONS: SCHEMA_VERSIONS,
   412	    createExperimentStore: createExperimentStore,
   413	    createOutcomeStore: createOutcomeStore,
   414	    createTimelineStore: createTimelineStore,
   415	    createFollowUpLinkStore: createFollowUpLinkStore,
   416	    createStoreMetadata: createStoreMetadata,
   417	  };
   418	  try { Object.freeze(api); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   419	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
   420	  if (root) {

   268	      if (!_wsHas(_authoritativeOutcomes, candidate)) return false;

   299	    } catch (e) { return true; }
   300	  }
   301	  function _nonEmptyStr(v) { return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0; }
   302	  function _isFiniteNumber(v) {
   303	    return typeof v === 'number' && _CAPTURED_NUMBER_IS_FINITE(v) === true;
   304	  }
   305	  function _isBool(v) { return v === true || v === false; }
   306	  function _idGrammarOk(s) {
   307	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(s)) return false;
   308	    if (ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(s)) return false;
   309	    if (!ID_GRAMMAR_RE.test(s)) return false;
   310	    return true;
   311	  }
   312	  // Codex E3-R1-02 closure: strict i18n-key grammar enforcement. driverFeedback (and any
   313	  // future i18n-keyed surface in the outcome) MUST pass this gate before being copied to
   314	  // the outcome envelope.
   315	  function _i18nKeyOk(s) {
   316	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(s)) return false;
   317	    if (s.length > I18N_KEY_MAX_LEN) return false;
   318	    if (!I18N_KEY_RE.test(s)) return false;
   319	    return true;
   320	  }
   321	  // Codex E3-R1-01 / E3-R5-02 closure: null-prototype membership set backed by a
   322	  // closure-local captured Array (not an object map). add() uses captured
   323	  // Array.prototype.push via captured Reflect.apply; has() uses captured indexOf.
   324	  // This eliminates the entire `defineProperty` attack surface from the set abstraction
   325	  // (a hostile pre-load defineProperty cannot inject phantom keys because we never
   326	  // call defineProperty for membership tracking). The set is shape-private: it is a
   327	  // plain array of string keys, never exposed beyond the classifier closure.
   328	  function _newCapturedNullSet() {
   329	    return [];
   330	  }
   331	  function _capturedSetHas(setObj, key) {
   332	    if (!_isPlainArray(setObj)) return false;
   333	    return _arrIndexOf(setObj, key) !== -1;
   334	  }
   335	  function _capturedSetAdd(setObj, key) {
   336	    if (!_isPlainArray(setObj)) return false;
   337	    if (_arrIndexOf(setObj, key) !== -1) return true;
   338	    return _arrPush(setObj, key);
   339	  }
   340	
   341	  // Deep freeze using captured Object.freeze + captured getOwnPropertyNames.
   342	  function _deepFreeze(v) {
   343	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return v;
   344	    try { _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE(v); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
   345	    var keys;
   346	    try { keys = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_NAMES(v); } catch (e) { return v; }
   347	    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
   348	      var k = keys[i];
   349	      var child;
   350	      try { child = v[k]; } catch (eGet) { continue; }
   351	      _deepFreeze(child);
   352	    }
   353	    return v;
   354	  }
   355	
   356	  // ---------- Input descriptor snapshot --------------------------------------------------------
   357	  // Mirrors D5 snapshotInputDescriptors: walks own descriptors (NOT [[Get]]) so a Proxy with
   358	  // hostile getters does NOT fire on first read. Symbol / non-enumerable / accessor / unknown
   359	  // own key → reject before any further inspection.
   360	  function _snapshotPlainShape(o, allowedKeys, invalidCode, opts) {
   361	    opts = opts || {};
   362	    if (!_isOriginalPlainObject(o)) {
   363	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], detail: 'not plain' };
   364	    }
   365	    if (opts.requireFrozen && _isFrozenSafe(o) !== true) {
   366	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'not frozen' };
   367	    }
   368	    if (_hasSymbolOwnKey(o)) {
   369	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], detail: 'symbol own key' };
   370	    }
   371	    var names;
   372	    try { names = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_NAMES(o); } catch (e) {
   373	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'descriptor read failed' };
   374	    }
   375	    var snap = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_CREATE(null);
   376	    for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
   377	      var k = names[i];
   378	      // Reject unknown own key — caller cannot smuggle `class`, `outcomeId`, `confounders`,
   379	      // etc. into the input wrapper.
   380	      if (_arrIndexOf(allowedKeys, k) === -1) {
   381	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], detail: 'unknown own key ' + k };
   382	      }
   383	      var d;
   384	      try { d = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(o, k); } catch (e) {
   385	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'descriptor inaccessible' };
   386	      }
   387	      if (!d || d.enumerable !== true) {
   388	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'non-enumerable key ' + k };
   389	      }
   390	      if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') {
   391	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'accessor key ' + k };
   392	      }
   393	      snap[k] = d.value;
   394	    }
   395	    return { valid: true, snapshot: snap };
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  // Codex E3-R1-03 closure: dense-array enforcement. Reject sparse arrays (any index in
   399	  // 0..length-1 missing an enumerable data descriptor → hole) AND reject any own key
   400	  // other than 'length' or a canonical numeric index (no Symbol keys, no string names,
   401	  // no negative / fractional / leading-zero indices). The walk indexes BY index, not by
   402	  // getOwnPropertyNames enumeration order, so a hostile array that puts named own keys
   403	  // first cannot smuggle a hole past the cap check.
   404	  function _snapshotArrayShape(a, invalidCode) {
   405	    if (!_isPlainArray(a)) {
   406	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'not array' };
   407	    }
   408	    if (_hasSymbolOwnKey(a)) {
   409	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], detail: 'symbol own key on array' };
   410	    }
   411	    var aLen;
   412	    try { aLen = a.length; } catch (e) {
   413	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'array length inaccessible' };
   414	    }
   415	    if (typeof aLen !== 'number' || !_CAPTURED_NUMBER_IS_INTEGER(aLen) || aLen < 0 || aLen > ARRAY_CAP) {
   416	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED], detail: 'array len ' + aLen };
   417	    }
   418	    // First sweep: enumerate own keys and reject ANY name other than 'length' or a
   419	    // canonical numeric index in [0, aLen). Reject Symbol keys (already above), reject
   420	    // non-enumerable / accessor / non-data descriptors on indices.
   421	    var names;
   422	    try { names = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_NAMES(a); } catch (e) {
   423	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'array desc inaccessible' };
   424	    }
   425	    for (var ni = 0; ni < names.length; ni++) {
   426	      var nm = names[ni];
   427	      if (nm === 'length') continue;
   428	      if (!ARRAY_INDEX_RE.test(nm)) {
   429	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], detail: 'non-index own key on array: ' + nm };
   430	      }
   431	      var idxN = +nm;
   432	      if (idxN < 0 || idxN >= aLen) {
   433	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'array index out of [0,length): ' + nm };
   434	      }
   435	      var dn;
   436	      try { dn = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(a, nm); } catch (e) {
   437	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'array elt desc fail' };
   438	      }
   439	      if (!dn || dn.enumerable !== true) {
   440	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'non-enumerable array elt at ' + nm };
   441	      }
   442	      if (typeof dn.get === 'function' || typeof dn.set === 'function') {
   443	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'accessor array elt at ' + nm };
   444	      }
   445	    }
   446	    // Second sweep: walk by index 0..aLen-1. Every index MUST have an OWN enumerable
   447	    // data descriptor (no holes, no prototype-inherited values).
   448	    var snap = [];
   449	    for (var i = 0; i < aLen; i++) {
   450	      var key = '' + i;
   451	      var d;
   452	      try { d = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(a, key); } catch (e) {
   453	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'array elt desc fail at ' + i };
   454	      }
   455	      if (!d) {
   456	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'sparse array hole at index ' + i };
   457	      }
   458	      if (d.enumerable !== true) {
   459	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'non-enumerable array elt at ' + i };
   460	      }
   461	      if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') {
   462	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [invalidCode], detail: 'accessor array elt at ' + i };
   463	      }
   464	      _arrPush(snap, d.value);
   465	    }
   466	    return { valid: true, snapshot: snap };
   467	  }
   468	
   469	  function _snapshotOpts(optsIn) {
   470	    var snap = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_CREATE(null);
   471	    if (optsIn === undefined || optsIn === null) return { valid: true, snapshot: snap };
   472	    var r = _snapshotPlainShape(optsIn, OPTS_KEYS_ALLOWED, CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID);
   473	    if (r.valid !== true) return r;
   474	    // Type-check each opt.
   475	    if (r.snapshot.clock !== undefined && r.snapshot.clock !== null && typeof r.snapshot.clock !== 'function') {
   476	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], detail: 'opts.clock not function' };
   477	    }
   478	    if (r.snapshot.referenceNowMs !== undefined && r.snapshot.referenceNowMs !== null
   479	        && typeof r.snapshot.referenceNowMs !== 'number') {
   480	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], detail: 'opts.referenceNowMs not number' };
   481	    }
   482	    if (r.snapshot.maxAgeMs !== undefined && r.snapshot.maxAgeMs !== null
   483	        && (typeof r.snapshot.maxAgeMs !== 'number' || !_CAPTURED_NUMBER_IS_INTEGER(r.snapshot.maxAgeMs) || r.snapshot.maxAgeMs < 0)) {
   484	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], detail: 'opts.maxAgeMs invalid' };
   485	    }
   486	    return { valid: true, snapshot: r.snapshot };
   487	  }
   488	
   489	  // ---------- Deterministic ID generator -------------------------------------------------------
   490	  // outcomeId is deterministic from (experimentId, changeId). No clock leak.
   491	  function _hash32(s) {
   492	    var h = 0x811c9dc5;
   493	    var len = typeof s === 'string' ? s.length : 0;
   494	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   495	      h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
   496	      h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
   497	    }
   498	    return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
   499	  }
   500	  function _fnv2(s) {
   501	    var slen = typeof s === 'string' ? s.length : 0;
   502	    return _hash32(s) + _hash32(s + '|' + slen);
   503	  }
   504	  function _outcomeId(experimentId, changeId) {
   505	    return 'outcome_' + _fnv2('outcome|' + experimentId + '|' + changeId);
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  // ---------- Main entry: classifyOutcome -----------------------------------------------------
   509	  /**
   510	   * classifyOutcome(input, opts) — produces a deep-frozen Outcome envelope.
   511	   *
   512	   * Ordered steps (clock MUST NOT fire before Step 9):
   513	   *   0.  intrinsic integrity (captures + contract surfaces still frozen)
   514	   *   1.  input wrapper descriptor snapshot (closed keys; symbol/accessor/proxy rejected)
   515	   *   2.  experiment frozen + E1 shape validation
   516	   *   3.  appliedChange snapshot + shape
   517	   *   4.  followUp snapshot + shape
   518	   *   5.  observation snapshot + shape
   519	   *   6.  controlVariableObservations snapshot + per-element E1 shape
   520	   *   7.  opts snapshot
   521	   *   8.  identity binding (status=applied, sourceExperimentId match, parentCaseId match)
   522	   *   9.  resolve freshness reference time (clock at most once)
   523	   *   10. freshness check on experiment.createdAt + appliedChange.appliedAt
   524	   *   11. comparison-validity computation (invalid_comparison precedence)
   525	   *   12. control-variable integrity (inconclusive_due_to_confounders precedence)
   526	   *   13. data-quality + direction/magnitude classification cascade
   527	   *   14. compose outcome
   528	   *   15. validate via E1 OUT.validateOutcomeShape
   529	   *   16. envelope byte cap
   530	   *   17. deep-freeze + register
   531	   */
   532	  function classifyOutcome(inputIn, optsIn) {
   533	    try {
   534	      // ---- Step 0: intrinsic integrity ----------------------------------------------------
   535	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
   536	        return _buildTamperBlock('intrinsic tampering detected at entry');
   537	      }
   538	
   539	      // ---- Step 1: input wrapper descriptor snapshot -------------------------------------
   540	      var inSnapR = _snapshotPlainShape(inputIn, INPUT_KEYS_ALLOWED, CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID, { requireFrozen: false });
   541	      if (inSnapR.valid !== true) {
   542	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult(inSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: inSnapR.detail });
   543	      }
   544	      var inputSnap = inSnapR.snapshot;
   545	
   546	      // ---- Step 2: experiment shape validation -------------------------------------------
   547	      // The Experiment must be deep-frozen (immutability) AND pass the E1 contract.
   548	      if (!_isFrozenSafe(inputSnap.experiment)) {
   549	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EXPERIMENT_INVALID], { detail: 'experiment not deep-frozen' });
   550	      }
   551	      var expCheck = EXP.validateExperimentShape(inputSnap.experiment);
   552	      if (expCheck.valid !== true) {
   553	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult(
   554	          expCheck.reasonCodes ? _arrSlice(expCheck.reasonCodes) : [CODES.EXPERIMENT_INVALID],
   555	          { detail: 'experiment shape invalid' }
   556	        );
   557	      }
   558	      var experiment = inputSnap.experiment;
   559	
   560	      // ---- Step 3: appliedChange snapshot + shape ----------------------------------------
   561	      var acSnapR = _snapshotPlainShape(inputSnap.appliedChange, APPLIED_CHANGE_KEYS_ALLOWED, CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID);
   562	      if (acSnapR.valid !== true) {
   563	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult(acSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: 'appliedChange: ' + acSnapR.detail });
   564	      }
   565	      var appliedChange = acSnapR.snapshot;
   566	      if (!_idGrammarOk(appliedChange.changeId)) {
   567	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'appliedChange.changeId invalid' });
   568	      }
   569	      if (!_idGrammarOk(appliedChange.sourceExperimentId)) {
   570	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'appliedChange.sourceExperimentId invalid' });
   571	      }
   572	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(appliedChange.appliedAt) || _isoToMs(appliedChange.appliedAt) === null) {
   573	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'appliedChange.appliedAt invalid' });
   574	      }
   575	
   576	      // ---- Step 4: followUp snapshot + shape ---------------------------------------------
   577	      var fuSnapR = _snapshotPlainShape(inputSnap.followUp, FOLLOWUP_KEYS_ALLOWED, CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID);
   578	      if (fuSnapR.valid !== true) {
   579	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult(fuSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: 'followUp: ' + fuSnapR.detail });
   580	      }
   581	      var followUp = fuSnapR.snapshot;
   582	      if (!_idGrammarOk(followUp.followUpCaseId)) {
   583	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID], { detail: 'followUp.followUpCaseId invalid' });
   584	      }
   585	      if (!_idGrammarOk(followUp.parentCaseId)) {
   586	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_PARENT_MISSING], { detail: 'followUp.parentCaseId invalid' });
   587	      }
   588	      if (followUp.parentCaseId === followUp.followUpCaseId) {
   589	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID], { detail: 'follow-up case is self-link' });
   590	      }
   591	      if (!_idGrammarOk(followUp.sessionId)) {
   592	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID], { detail: 'followUp.sessionId invalid' });
   593	      }
   594	      if (!_idGrammarOk(followUp.parentSessionId)) {
   595	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID], { detail: 'followUp.parentSessionId invalid' });
   596	      }
   597	      if (!_isBool(followUp.hasExplicitReference)) {
   598	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'followUp.hasExplicitReference not bool' });
   599	      }
   600	      if (!_isFiniteNumber(followUp.comparabilityScore) || followUp.comparabilityScore < 0 || followUp.comparabilityScore > 1) {
   601	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_COMPARABILITY_INSUFFICIENT], { detail: 'followUp.comparabilityScore out of [0,1]' });
   602	      }
   603	
   604	      // ---- Step 5: observation snapshot + shape ------------------------------------------
   605	      var obsSnapR = _snapshotPlainShape(inputSnap.observation, OBSERVATION_KEYS_ALLOWED, CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID);
   606	      if (obsSnapR.valid !== true) {
   607	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult(obsSnapR.reasonCodes, { detail: 'observation: ' + obsSnapR.detail });
   608	      }
   609	      var observation = obsSnapR.snapshot;
   610	      // observedDirection: enum OR null
   611	      if (observation.observedDirection !== null && _arrIndexOf(DIRECTION_ALLOWED, observation.observedDirection) === -1) {
   612	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.observedDirection invalid' });
   613	      }
   614	      // observedMagnitude: finite OR null
   615	      if (observation.observedMagnitude !== null && !_isFiniteNumber(observation.observedMagnitude)) {
   616	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.observedMagnitude invalid' });
   617	      }
   618	      // driverFeedback: STRICT i18n key (dotted lower_snake_case, 2..6 segments, ≤128 chars)
   619	      // OR null. Codex E3-R1-02 closure: a non-empty-string check alone permits free-text
   620	      // blame / causation / paths to be laundered into the outcome envelope. The strict

  1067	          // Worst-case-ish digit count: 24 covers JSON-canonical doubles.
  1068	          var s = (v).toString();
  1069	          return s.length;
  1070	        }
  1071	        if (t === 'string') {
  1072	          // 2 quotes + escape-conservative 6x byte-per-char ceiling for unicode escapes.
  1073	          return 2 + _utf8Bytes(v) + 0;
  1074	        }
  1075	        if (_CAPTURED_ARRAY_IS_ARRAY(v)) {
  1076	          var aLen = v.length;
  1077	          if (typeof aLen !== 'number' || aLen < 0) return Infinity;
  1078	          var sum = 2; // []
  1079	          if (aLen > 0) sum += aLen - 1; // commas
  1080	          for (var ai = 0; ai < aLen; ai++) {
  1081	            // Direct index access on a frozen array is safe (frozen blocks mutation).
  1082	            var c = _byteSize(v[ai], depth + 1);
  1083	            if (c === Infinity) return Infinity;
  1084	            sum += c;
  1085	          }
  1086	          return sum;
  1087	        }
  1088	        if (t === 'object') {
  1089	          // Plain object only — walk via captured getOwnPropertyNames so prototype chain
  1090	          // and toJSON cannot interfere.
  1091	          var names;
  1092	          try { names = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_NAMES(v); } catch (eDesc) { return Infinity; }
  1093	          var oSum = 2; // {}
  1094	          var counted = 0;
  1095	          for (var ki = 0; ki < names.length; ki++) {
  1096	            var kk = names[ki];
  1097	            var d;
  1098	            try { d = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(v, kk); } catch (eDesc2) { return Infinity; }
  1099	            if (!d || d.enumerable !== true) continue;
  1100	            if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') return Infinity;
  1101	            if (counted > 0) oSum += 1; // comma
  1102	            oSum += 2 + _utf8Bytes(kk) + 1; // "key":
  1103	            var cv = _byteSize(d.value, depth + 1);
  1104	            if (cv === Infinity) return Infinity;
  1105	            oSum += cv;
  1106	            counted++;
  1107	          }
  1108	          return oSum;
  1109	        }
  1110	        return Infinity;
  1111	      }
  1112	      var envelopeBytes;
  1113	      try { envelopeBytes = _byteSize(outcome, 0); }
  1114	      catch (e) { envelopeBytes = Infinity; }
  1115	      if (envelopeBytes === Infinity || envelopeBytes > ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP) {
  1116	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'outcome envelope ' + envelopeBytes + ' bytes > ' + ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP });
  1117	      }
  1118	      // Defence-in-depth: re-check the array lengths did not change during the walk.
  1119	      if (outcome.confounders.length !== preBytePushSizes.confounders
  1120	          || outcome.limitations.length !== preBytePushSizes.limitations
  1121	          || outcome.sideEffects.length !== preBytePushSizes.sideEffects
  1122	          || outcome.dataQualityIssues.length !== preBytePushSizes.dataQualityIssues) {
  1123	        return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.OUTCOME_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'outcome array length mutated during byte-size walk (toJSON attack suspected)' });
  1124	      }
  1125	
  1126	      // ---- Step 18: re-check intrinsics post-classify, register --------------------------
  1127	      if (!_intrinsicsIntact()) {
  1128	        return _buildTamperBlock('intrinsic tampering detected post-classify');
  1129	      }
  1130	      _registerAuthoritativeOutcome(outcome);
  1131	
  1132	      return _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE({ valid: true, outcome: outcome });
  1133	
  1134	    } catch (eOuter) {
  1135	      return RC_E.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'classifyOutcome threw on hostile input' });
  1136	    }
  1137	  }
  1138	
  1139	  // ---------- Public API ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  1140	  var api = {
  1141	    SERVICE_VERSION: SERVICE_VERSION,
  1142	    OUTCOME_SCHEMA_VERSION: OUTCOME_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  1143	    ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP: ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP,
  1144	    MIN_COMPARABILITY_FOR_VALID_COMPARISON: MIN_COMPARABILITY_FOR_VALID_COMPARISON,
  1145	    DATA_QUALITY_ISSUE_BLOCK_THRESHOLD: DATA_QUALITY_ISSUE_BLOCK_THRESHOLD,
  1146	    classifyOutcome: classifyOutcome,
  1147	    verifyAuthoritativeOutcome: verifyAuthoritativeOutcome,
  1148	  };
  1149	  _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE(api);
  1150	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  1151	    module.exports = api;
  1152	  } else if (root) {
  1153	    try { Object.defineProperty(root, 'R3_0E_OutcomeClassifier', { value: api, writable: false, enumerable: false, configurable: false }); }
  1154	    catch (e) { root.R3_0E_OutcomeClassifier = api; }
  1155	  }
  1156	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

codex
The classifier mostly enforces the requested precedence and authority checks, but I’m seeing one risk around deterministic ID padding that I need to confirm against output validation and privacy constraints. I’m checking timeline next before deciding whether that rises to a gate finding.

   268	  // ---------- Deterministic ID generators ----------------------------------------------------
   269	  // Codex E4-R5-01 closure: capture-free hex-padding. Both Number.prototype.toString
   270	  // and String.prototype.padStart could be rebound post-load to subvert deterministic
   271	  // id derivation. Manual 8-char hex emission via local lookup table is
   272	  // tamper-immune (uses only basic operators + string concatenation).
   273	  function _toHex8(h) {
   274	    var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
   275	    var out = '';
   276	    var x = h >>> 0;
   277	    for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
   278	      var nibble = (x >>> (i * 4)) & 0xF;
   279	      // Bracket access on a string primitive resolves via internal slot indexed-access,
   280	      // avoiding ambient String.prototype.charAt.
   281	      out += hexChars[nibble];
   282	    }
   283	    return out;
   284	  }
   285	  function _hash32(s) {
   286	    var h = 0x811c9dc5;
   287	    var len = typeof s === 'string' ? s.length : 0;
   288	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   289	      // Codex E4-R6-01 closure: captured charCodeAt prevents post-load tampering
   290	      // from changing deterministic id derivation.
   291	      h ^= _charCodeAt(s, i);
   292	      h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
   293	    }
   294	    return _toHex8(h);
   295	  }
   296	  function _fnv2(s) {

   341	  function _resolveClock(optsIn) {
   342	    var snap;
   343	    if (optsIn === undefined || optsIn === null) snap = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_CREATE(null);
   344	    else {
   345	      var r = _snapshotPlain(optsIn, OPTS_KEYS, CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID);
   346	      if (r.valid !== true) return { valid: false, reasonCodes: r.reasonCodes, detail: r.detail };
   347	      snap = r.snapshot;
   348	      if (snap.clock !== undefined && snap.clock !== null && typeof snap.clock !== 'function') {
   349	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], detail: 'opts.clock not function' };
   350	      }
   351	      if (snap.referenceNowMs !== undefined && snap.referenceNowMs !== null
   352	          && (typeof snap.referenceNowMs !== 'number' || !_CAPTURED_NUMBER_IS_INTEGER(snap.referenceNowMs) || snap.referenceNowMs < 0)) {
   353	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], detail: 'opts.referenceNowMs invalid' };
   354	      }
   355	    }
   356	    var refMs = null;
   357	    var iso = null;
   358	    if (typeof snap.referenceNowMs === 'number') {
   359	      refMs = snap.referenceNowMs;
   360	      // Codex E4-R2-01 closure: canonicalization via captured intrinsics with round-trip
   361	      // verification. Post-load Date / Date.prototype.toISOString mutation cannot affect.
   362	      iso = _canonicalIso(refMs);
   363	    } else if (typeof snap.clock === 'function') {
   364	      var ciso = null;
   365	      try { ciso = snap.clock(); } catch (e) { ciso = null; }

   370	          iso = _canonicalIso(ms);
   371	        }
   372	      }
   373	    }
   374	    if (typeof refMs !== 'number' || refMs !== refMs || iso === null) {
   375	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], detail: 'cannot resolve clock' };

   377	    return { valid: true, refMs: refMs, iso: iso };

   380	  // ---------- E4 timeline-event params validator (shared by append + project) ---------------
   381	  // Codex E4-R1-01 closure: projectTimeline MUST re-apply the SAME primitive allowlist
   382	  // used by appendTimelineEvent. The E1 contract is structurally permissive (params
   383	  // accepts any plain object); the E4 service layer adds the strict allowlist. A
   384	  // corrupted stored event with hostile params must NOT survive into an authoritative
   385	  // projection.
   386	  function _validateEventParams(params) {
   387	    if (params === null || params === undefined) return { valid: true, value: null };
   388	    if (!_isOriginalPlainObject(params)) return { valid: false, code: 'params not plain' };
   389	    if (_hasSymbolOwnKey(params)) return { valid: false, code: 'params has Symbol key' };
   390	    var names;
   391	    try { names = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_NAMES(params); }
   392	    catch (e) { return { valid: false, code: 'params desc fail' }; }
   393	    if (names.length > 16) return { valid: false, code: 'params keys exceed cap' };
   394	    var rebuilt = {};
   395	    for (var pi = 0; pi < names.length; pi++) {
   396	      var pk = names[pi];
   397	      // 'correction_of' is the canonical reserved key — its value is a strict id.
   398	      var keyOk = _reTest(SOBER_PARAM_KEY_RE, pk);
   399	      if (!keyOk) return { valid: false, code: 'params key not sober: ' + pk };
   400	      // Codex E4-R2-03 closure: even sober ids that match reserved hostile tokens (blame,
   401	      // cause, driver_error, ...) are forbidden in BOTH keys and string values. Narrative
   402	      // / causal / blame content must travel through i18n keys whose translations live
   403	      // outside the runtime — never as bare strings in the event params.
   404	      if (_containsReservedHostileToken(pk)) return { valid: false, code: 'params key matches reserved hostile token: ' + pk };
   405	      var pd;
   406	      try { pd = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(params, pk); }
   407	      catch (e) { return { valid: false, code: 'params desc fail at ' + pk }; }
   408	      if (!pd || pd.enumerable !== true) return { valid: false, code: 'params non-enum at ' + pk };
   409	      if (typeof pd.get === 'function' || typeof pd.set === 'function') {
   410	        return { valid: false, code: 'params accessor at ' + pk };
   411	      }
   412	      var pv = pd.value;
   413	      if (pv === null) {
   414	        // allowed
   415	      } else if (typeof pv === 'boolean') {
   416	        // allowed
   417	      } else if (typeof pv === 'number') {
   418	        if (!_isFiniteNumber(pv)) return { valid: false, code: 'params[' + pk + '] not finite' };
   419	      } else if (typeof pv === 'string') {
   420	        if (!_i18nKeyOk(pv) && !_idGrammarOk(pv)) {
   421	          return { valid: false, code: 'params[' + pk + '] string not strict i18n key / id' };
   422	        }
   423	        // Codex E4-R2-03 closure: reserved hostile tokens in string values are forbidden
   424	        // regardless of grammar match.
   425	        if (_containsReservedHostileToken(pv)) {
   426	          return { valid: false, code: 'params[' + pk + '] string matches reserved hostile token' };
   427	        }
   428	      } else {
   429	        return { valid: false, code: 'params[' + pk + '] not primitive (string/number/boolean/null)' };
   430	      }
   431	      try {
   432	        _CAPTURED_OBJECT_DEFINE_PROPERTY(rebuilt, pk, { value: pv, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true });
   433	      } catch (e) {
   434	        return { valid: false, code: 'params rebuild failed at ' + pk };
   435	      }
   436	      // Codex E4-R1-03 / E3-R4-01 closure pattern: re-read descriptor and verify value
   437	      // identity with Object.is. Defeats pre-load defineProperty poisoning.
   438	      var dCheck;
   439	      try { dCheck = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(rebuilt, pk); }
   440	      catch (e) { return { valid: false, code: 'params re-read fail' }; }
   441	      if (!dCheck || dCheck.enumerable !== true || typeof dCheck.get === 'function' || typeof dCheck.set === 'function') {
   442	        return { valid: false, code: 'params post-define corrupt at ' + pk };
   443	      }
   444	      var same = false;
   445	      try { same = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS(dCheck.value, pv) === true; } catch (e) { same = false; }
   446	      if (!same) return { valid: false, code: 'params post-define value mismatch at ' + pk };
   447	    }
   448	    return { valid: true, value: rebuilt };
   449	  }

   519	    function getFollowUpLink(linkId) {
   520	      if (!_idGrammarOk(linkId)) return Promise.resolve(null);
   521	      return followUpLinkStore.get(linkId).then(function (rec) {
   522	        if (rec === null || rec === undefined) return null;

   547	    function listFollowUpLinksForParent(parentCaseId) {
   548	      if (!_idGrammarOk(parentCaseId)) return Promise.resolve([]);
   549	      // Use the store's listForParent (E2 already validates each row and enforces
   550	      // parent membership). Re-wrap each into our authority registry.
   551	      if (typeof followUpLinkStore.listForParent !== 'function') return Promise.resolve([]);
   552	      return followUpLinkStore.listForParent(parentCaseId).then(function (rows) {
   553	        var LINK_KEYS_FROZEN = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE(['schemaVersion', 'linkId', 'parentCaseId', 'followUpCaseId', 'experimentId', 'parentStatus', 'createdAt']);
   554	        var out = [];
   555	        for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
   556	          // Codex E4-R4-01 closure: pass each row through structuredClone (or JSON
   557	          // clone fallback) BEFORE descriptor snapshot. structuredClone produces a
   558	          // brand-new plain-object tree — Proxy traps fire during the clone but the
   559	          // result is concrete data; no Proxy survives. The subsequent descriptor
   560	          // snapshot operates on the clean tree.
   561	          var cleaned = _cleanClone(rows[i]);
   562	          if (cleaned === null) continue;
   563	          // Codex E4-R2-02 closure: apply the SAME descriptor snapshot used by
   564	          // getFollowUpLink. Only data descriptors with the closed key set survive.
   565	          var snap = _snapshotPlain(cleaned, LINK_KEYS_FROZEN, CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
   566	          if (snap.valid !== true) continue;

   568	            schemaVersion: snap.snapshot.schemaVersion,
   569	            linkId: snap.snapshot.linkId,
   570	            parentCaseId: snap.snapshot.parentCaseId,
   571	            followUpCaseId: snap.snapshot.followUpCaseId,
   572	            experimentId: snap.snapshot.experimentId,
   573	            parentStatus: snap.snapshot.parentStatus,
   574	            createdAt: snap.snapshot.createdAt,
   575	          };
   576	          var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(rebuilt);
   577	          if (v.valid !== true) continue;
   578	          _deepFreeze(rebuilt);
   579	          _registerAuthoritativeLink(rebuilt);
   580	          _arrPush(out, rebuilt);
   581	        }
   582	        return _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE(out);
   583	      });
   584	    }
   585	
   586	    // ---- appendTimelineEvent ------------------------------------------------------------
   587	    function appendTimelineEvent(inputIn, optsIn) {
   588	      // Step 1: descriptor snapshot
   589	      var inR = _snapshotPlain(inputIn, EVENT_INPUT_KEYS, CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID);
   590	      if (inR.valid !== true) return Promise.resolve(_block(inR.reasonCodes, inR.detail));
   591	      var i = inR.snapshot;
   592	
   593	      // Step 2: structural validation
   594	      if (!_idGrammarOk(i.caseId)) return Promise.resolve(_block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'caseId invalid'));
   595	      if (_arrIndexOf(EVENT_KIND_ALLOWED, i.kind) === -1) {
   596	        return Promise.resolve(_block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'kind not in closed enum: ' + i.kind));
   597	      }
   598	      if (!_i18nKeyOk(i.i18nKey)) return Promise.resolve(_block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'i18nKey not strict i18n key'));
   599	      // Codex E4-R1-01/03 closure: delegate params validation to the shared E4 helper
   600	      // which applies the primitive allowlist AND the post-define value-equality re-read.
   601	      var pvR = _validateEventParams(i.params);
   602	      if (pvR.valid !== true) return Promise.resolve(_block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'params: ' + pvR.code));
   603	      var rebuiltParams = pvR.value;
   604	      // Correction event: correctionOf must be an existing event id (validated grammar)
   605	      // OR null/undefined. The store layer enforces append-only — the original event is
   606	      // never modified.
   607	      if (i.correctionOf !== undefined && i.correctionOf !== null) {
   608	        if (!_idGrammarOk(i.correctionOf)) return Promise.resolve(_block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'correctionOf invalid id'));
   609	      }
   610	
   611	      // Step 3: opts + clock (post-authority)
   612	      var ck = _resolveClock(optsIn);
   613	      if (ck.valid !== true) return Promise.resolve(_block(ck.reasonCodes, ck.detail));
   614	
   615	      // Step 4: fetch existing timeline to derive sequence (post-authority)
   616	      return timelineStore.getTimeline(i.caseId).then(function (existing) {
   617	        var existingEvents = (existing && _isArraySafe(existing.events)) ? existing.events : [];
   618	        var sequence = existingEvents.length;
   619	        // Codex E4-R1-05 closure: STRICT monotonicity. The E1 contract spec is monotonic
   620	        // by createdAt; equal-timestamp appends are ambiguous (the deterministic eventId
   621	        // is derived from sequence which IS unique, but two events at the same ms can
   622	        // still mask the ordering on reload). Reject refMs <= prev so every event is
   623	        // strictly later than the previous.
   624	        if (existingEvents.length > 0) {
   625	          var prevMs = _isoToMs(existingEvents[existingEvents.length - 1].createdAt);
   626	          if (prevMs !== null && ck.refMs <= prevMs) {
   627	            return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_ORDERING_INVALID], 'clock not strictly monotonic: refMs ' + ck.refMs + ' <= prev ' + prevMs);
   628	          }
   629	        }
   630	
   631	        // Codex E4-R1-04 closure: correctionOf MUST reference an existing event id in
   632	        // THIS case's timeline. Otherwise a caller could correct a non-existent event,
   633	        // leaving an orphaned reference in the persisted timeline.
   634	        if (i.correctionOf !== undefined && i.correctionOf !== null) {
   635	          var found = false;
   636	          for (var ei = 0; ei < existingEvents.length; ei++) {
   637	            if (existingEvents[ei].eventId === i.correctionOf) { found = true; break; }
   638	          }
   639	          if (!found) {
   640	            return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'correctionOf references non-existent event: ' + i.correctionOf);
   641	          }
   642	        }
   643	
   644	        // Step 5: compose event
   645	        var eventIdStr = _eventId(i.caseId, sequence, i.kind, i.i18nKey);
   646	        if (_utf8Bytes(eventIdStr) > EVENT_ID_BYTE_CAP) {
   647	          return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID, CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], 'eventId byte cap exceeded');
   648	        }
   649	        // If params include a correctionOf reference, store it in params under canonical key.
   650	        var finalParams = rebuiltParams;
   651	        if (i.correctionOf !== undefined && i.correctionOf !== null) {
   652	          if (finalParams === null) finalParams = {};
   653	          try {
   654	            _CAPTURED_OBJECT_DEFINE_PROPERTY(finalParams, 'correction_of', { value: i.correctionOf, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true });
   655	          } catch (e) {
   656	            return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID], 'correctionOf injection failed');
   657	          }
   658	          // Codex E4-R1-03 closure: post-define value-equality re-read for correction_of.
   659	          var dCO;
   660	          try { dCO = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_GET_OWN_DESC(finalParams, 'correction_of'); }
   661	          catch (eRR) { return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], 'correction_of re-read fail'); }
   662	          if (!dCO || dCO.enumerable !== true || typeof dCO.get === 'function' || typeof dCO.set === 'function') {
   663	            return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], 'correction_of post-define corrupt');
   664	          }
   665	          var coSame = false;
   666	          try { coSame = _CAPTURED_OBJECT_IS(dCO.value, i.correctionOf) === true; } catch (eRR2) { coSame = false; }
   667	          if (!coSame) {
   668	            return _block([CODES.TIMELINE_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], 'correction_of post-define value mismatch');
   669	          }
   670	        }
   671	        var event = {
   672	          eventId: eventIdStr,
   673	          kind: i.kind,
   674	          createdAt: ck.iso,
   675	          i18nKey: i.i18nKey,
   676	          params: finalParams,
   677	        };
   678	
   679	        // Step 6: persist via E2 store (E2 enforces duplicate + out-of-order + future-schema)
   680	        return timelineStore.appendEvent(i.caseId, event).then(function () {

   111	  function _ownKeysEqual(o, expected) {

   198	    // Codex E5-R1-03 closure: ignore injected verifier deps entirely. Production code

   216	    function _makeState(opts) {
   217	      var state = {
   218	        schemaVersion: VIEWMODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   219	        displayState: opts.displayState,
   220	        caseId: opts.caseId || null,
   221	        sessionId: opts.sessionId || null,
   222	        outcome: opts.outcome || null,
   223	        outcomeClass: opts.outcomeClass || null,
   224	        outcomeClassI18nKey: opts.outcomeClassI18nKey || null,
   225	        projection: opts.projection || null,
   226	        projectionEventCount: typeof opts.projectionEventCount === 'number' ? opts.projectionEventCount : 0,
   227	        links: opts.links || _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE([]),
   228	        linksCount: typeof opts.linksCount === 'number' ? opts.linksCount : 0,
   229	        activation: opts.activation,
   230	        disclaimers: _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE([
   231	          'r3.0e.disclaimer.no_causation',
   232	          'r3.0e.disclaimer.no_driver_blame',
   233	          'r3.0e.disclaimer.no_auto_setup',
   234	          'r3.0e.disclaimer.same_case_session_only',
   235	        ]),
   236	      };
   237	      _deepFreeze(state);
   238	      _registerAuthoritativeState(state);
   239	      return state;
   240	    }

   280	          rationaleParams: _CAPTURED_OBJECT_FREEZE({}),
   281	        }),
   282	      });
   283	    }

   285	    /**
   286	     * derive({outcome, projection, links}) — produces an authoritative viewmodel state.
   287	     *
   288	     * Inputs:
   289	     *   outcome    — an authoritative outcome envelope from classifier.classifyOutcome
   290	     *   projection — an authoritative timeline projection from timelineService.projectTimeline
   291	     *   links      — an array of authoritative follow-up link records (each from createFollowUpLink
   292	     *                or listFollowUpLinksForParent)
   293	     *
   294	     * Any input failing its verifier → error-sanitized state. This is the SAME pattern as
   295	     * the D5 brief: refuse to mix authoritative + non-authoritative content.
   296	     */
   297	    function derive(inputIn) {
   298	      try {
   299	        if (!inputIn || typeof inputIn !== 'object') return error();
   300	        var outcome = inputIn.outcome || null;
   301	        var projection = inputIn.projection || null;
   302	        var links = inputIn.links || [];
   303	
   304	        // Authority gates.
   305	        if (outcome !== null && classifier.verifyAuthoritativeOutcome(outcome) !== true) return error();
   306	        if (projection !== null && timelineService.verifyAuthoritativeTimelineProjection(projection) !== true) return error();
   307	        if (!_CAPTURED_ARRAY_IS_ARRAY(links)) return error();
   308	        for (var li = 0; li < links.length; li++) {
   309	          if (timelineService.verifyAuthoritativeFollowUpLink(links[li]) !== true) return error();
   310	        }
   311	
   312	        // Cross-binding: if outcome AND projection both present, they should refer to
   313	        // the same case (projection.caseId === <some link's parentCaseId> OR outcome's
   314	        // experimentId is in the projection's events; we keep the binding LOOSE here
   315	        // because the data model has no direct outcome→projection link).
   316	        var caseId = projection !== null ? projection.caseId : null;
   317	        var sessionId = null; // outcome envelope does not currently carry sessionId; UI shows it from links
   318	
   319	        var outcomeClass = outcome !== null ? outcome['class'] : null;
   320	        // Codex E5-R1-03 closure: outcomeClass must be in the closed allowlist.

   108	  function _byOrder(a, b) { return (a.order || 0) - (b.order || 0); }
   109	  function _nodes(kind) { return Object.keys(NAV_NODES).map(function (k) { return NAV_NODES[k]; }).filter(function (n) { return n.kind === kind; }).sort(_byOrder); }
   110	  function _features() { return Object.keys(FEATURES).map(function (k) { return FEATURES[k]; }); }

   112	  function deriveMainNav() { return _nodes('shell_section').map(function (n) { var o = { id: n.id, labelKey: n.labelKey }; if (n.availability === 'deferred') o.deferred = n.deferredReason; return o; }); }
   113	  function deriveCaseNav() { return _nodes('case_subview').map(function (n) { return { id: n.subviewId, labelKey: n.labelKey }; }); }
   114	  function deriveCaseSubviewIds() { return _nodes('case_subview').map(function (n) { return n.subviewId; }); }

   116	    return _nodes('setuplib_area').map(function (area) {
   117	      var feats = _features().filter(function (f) { return f.navNodeId === area.id && f.entryPoints && f.entryPoints.desktop === true; })
   118	        .map(function (f) { return { id: f.id, labelKey: f.labelKey, descriptionKey: f.descriptionKey, availability: f.availability, allowedActions: f.allowedActions }; });
   119	      return { areaId: area.id, labelKey: area.labelKey, features: feats };
   120	    });

   122	  // the pane-id whitelist for the setup_library section — DERIVED (replaces the inline array; INCLUDES 'predict')
   123	  function deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds() {
   124	    var set = {}; _features().forEach(function (f) { if ((f.area === 'vehicle_setup' || f.area === 'engineering_tools' || f.area === 'analysis_support') && f.rendererAdapter) set[f.rendererAdapter.paneId] = true; });
   125	    return Object.keys(set);
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  function getFeature(id) { return FEATURES[id] || null; }
   129	  function getNode(id) { return NAV_NODES[id] || null; }
   130	
   131	  // NOTE: featureId → legacy UI-state (shellSection/currentTab/caseSubview) is constructed ONLY in feature-router.js
   132	  // (the single translator). The registry stays declarative data (FEATURES/NAV_NODES) + navigation derivations.
   133	
   134	  function getFeatureBreadcrumb(featureId) {
   135	    var f = FEATURES[featureId]; if (!f) return [];
   136	    var crumbs = [], node = NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId];
   137	    if (node && node.kind === 'setuplib_area') { crumbs.push({ id: 'setup_library', labelKey: NAV_NODES.setup_library.labelKey }); crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   138	    else if (node && node.parentNodeId) { var p = NAV_NODES[node.parentNodeId]; if (p) crumbs.push({ id: p.id, labelKey: p.labelKey }); crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   139	    else if (node) { crumbs.push({ id: node.id, labelKey: node.labelKey }); }
   140	    crumbs.push({ id: f.id, labelKey: f.labelKey });
   141	    return crumbs;
   142	  }
   143	  function isFeatureReachable(featureId) {
   144	    var f = FEATURES[featureId]; if (!f) return false;
   145	    if (f.availability === 'deferred') return true; // reachable as an explicit deferred panel
   146	    return !!(f.navNodeId && NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId] && (f.rendererAdapter || f.area === 'deferred'));
   147	  }
   148	  function getFeatureEntryPoints(featureId) { var f = FEATURES[featureId]; return f ? f.entryPoints : null; }
   149	
   150	  // registry self-validation (used by contract tests)
   151	  function validateRegistry() {
   152	    var errors = [];
   153	    var ids = Object.keys(FEATURES);
   154	    var seen = {};
   155	    ids.forEach(function (id) {
   156	      var f = FEATURES[id];
   157	      if (f.id !== id) errors.push('id_mismatch:' + id);
   158	      if (seen[id]) errors.push('duplicate_id:' + id); seen[id] = true;
   159	      ['id', 'area', 'labelKey', 'availability', 'navNodeId', 'entryPoints'].forEach(function (k) { if (f[k] === undefined || f[k] === null) errors.push('missing_field:' + id + '.' + k); });
   160	      if (!NAV_NODES[f.navNodeId]) errors.push('dangling_navNode:' + id + '->' + f.navNodeId);
   161	      if (f.availability === 'deferred') { if (!f.deferredReason) errors.push('deferred_no_reason:' + id); }
   162	      else if (!f.rendererAdapter) errors.push('no_renderer_adapter:' + id);
   163	      if (f.availability !== 'deferred' && !isFeatureReachable(id)) errors.push('unreachable:' + id);
   164	    });
   165	    return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors: errors };
   166	  }
   167	
   168	  var api = {
   169	    FEATURES: FEATURES, NAV_NODES: NAV_NODES,
   170	    getFeature: getFeature, getNode: getNode,
   171	    deriveMainNav: deriveMainNav, deriveCaseNav: deriveCaseNav, deriveCaseSubviewIds: deriveCaseSubviewIds,
   172	    deriveSetupLibraryAreas: deriveSetupLibraryAreas, deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds: deriveSetupLibraryPaneIds,
   173	    getFeatureBreadcrumb: getFeatureBreadcrumb,
   174	    isFeatureReachable: isFeatureReachable, getFeatureEntryPoints: getFeatureEntryPoints,
   175	    validateRegistry: validateRegistry,
   176	  };
   177	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
   178	  if (root) root.FeatureRegistry = api;
   179	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

codex
Governance state and feature registry line up with E5 activation, and the R3.0B case-record schema has no diff against main. I’m going to run a narrow adversarial check for the classifier ID derivation path because the code uses ambient `padStart` where the E4 service already uses manual hex padding.
codex

codex
The classifier check reproduced the concern: after module load, rebinding `String.prototype.padStart` lets `classifyOutcome` produce and register an authoritative outcome with a filesystem-looking `outcomeId`. That is a gate blocker because it violates the E3 closure claim and the E3/E5 privacy surface.

Finding R3.0E-E-GATE-01: E3 outcomeId derivation still trusts ambient `String.prototype.padStart`, allowing post-load tampering to inject path-like data into an authoritative outcome.
  evidence: renderer/js/r3-0e-outcome-classifier.js:498
  evidence: contracts/r3.0e/outcome-contract.js:68
  severity: BLOCK
  proposed closure: replace E3 `_hash32` hex formatting with the same manual `_toHex8` pattern used by E4, and/or enforce a closed `outcome_[0-9a-f]{16}` grammar in `validateOutcomeShape` and `verifyAuthoritativeOutcome`. Add an adversarial test that rebinds `String.prototype.padStart` after module load and expects BLOCK or canonical hex output.
